QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version\n// \n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n      } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Generate synthetic data\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n    \n    // Perform QT clustering\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = cluster_end - cluster_start);\n    \n    printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n    printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n    \n    // Calculate statistics and static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n    \n    const double avg_cluster_size =       100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);\n    printf(\"Average cluster size: %.2f\\n\", avg_cluster_size);\n    printf(\"Maximum cluster size: %d\\n\", max_cluster_size);\n    \n    // Performance metrics\n    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;\n    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;\n    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\", \n           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        //  }\n        }\n        for (int m : membership) {\n            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));\n        }\n        print_results(membershipData, \"ClusterMembership\");\n    }\n QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version\\n// \\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\\n\\n#include <algorithm>\\n#include <chrono>\\n#include <cmath>\\n#include <cstdio>\\n#include <cstdlib>\\n#include <cstring>\\n#include <limits>\\n#include <vector>\\n\\n#include \\\"../common/results_output.hpp\\\"\\n\\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\\n\\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\\nstruct Point {\\n    double x, y;\\n};\\n\\n// Structure to represent a cluster\\nstruct Cluster {\\n    std::vector<int> members;\\n    int seed_point;\\n};\\n\\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\\n    \\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\\n    int count = 0;\\n    \\n    while (count < N) {\\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\\n        \\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\\n            group_cnt = N - count;\\n        }\\n        \\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\\n            const double r = frand() * R;\\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\\n            \\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\\n                continue;\\n            }\\n            \\n            points[count] = {x, y};\\n            count++;\\n            group_cnt--;\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\\n}\\n\\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n                     const double threshold,\\n                     const int point_count) {\\n    int closest_point = -1;\\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\\n    \\n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\\n        \\n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\\n        }\\n        \\n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\\n            closest_point = candidate;\\n        }\\n    }\\n    \\n    return closest_point;\\n}\\n\\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n                              const double threshold,\\n                              const int point_count,\\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\\n    std::vector<int> members;\\n    \\n    // Add seed point\\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\\n Need equivalent semantics.\n\nUnderstand algorithm deeply to parallelize while preserving exact output/hash semantics.\n\nQT clustering:\n- outer while unclustered_indices nonempty:\n  - for each unclustered seed (in increasing index order because unclustered_indices sorted), generate candidate cluster\n  - generateCandidateCluster: starts members [seed], then repeatedly findClosestPoint: scans candidates 0..N-1 ascending, skipping clustered or in_cluster, calculates max distance to current members (using double sqrt), requires max_dist < threshold and < current min_diameter; updates when strictly less. Note candidates ascending means ties choose smallest index.\n  - If cardinality > max_cardinality, updates best (strictly greater). Seeds in ascending order, so ties choose first seed (smallest index).\n  - add best cluster, mark clustered, remove.\n\nThis is highly sequential outer loop, but candidate cluster generation for different seeds can be parallelized. Each candidate cluster growth is sequential (each addition depends on prior). But computing distances from candidate to members is O(m*N) each step; total may be huge.\n\nPotential GPU parallelization:\n1. Precompute pairwise distance matrix (N x N) once. But candidate \"max distance to all members\" can be computed via matrix row max over member indices. This can use GPU. But N maybe large (default 1000; benchmark likely tests e.g. 1000, 2000, 5000, maybe 10000). Storing N^2 doubles: 10000^2*8 = 800MB; okay on 24GB. But unknown max. Could use float? Semantics require double distance comparisons and exact result. Need equivalent semantics likely hash of memberships. If computing distances in double, sqrt. Precomputing exact double distances preserves.\n\n2. Parallelize across seeds: each thread block handles one seed, sequentially grows candidate cluster with parallel reduction over candidates. This matches semantics exactly. Outer loop picks best cardinality; ties by seed order. Number of seeds per iteration can be large, so parallelism good initially but decreases—outer iteration count clusters maybe ~? For 1000 points, number of clusters likely dozens, so each round lots of seeds. Early rounds dominate.\n\n3. Parallelize findClosestPoint across candidates via GPU thread per candidate; each computes max over members (member count grows). This is O(N*m) per addition. Candidate generation is O(N*m^2) per seed roughly? Actually sum over additions of N*m. For clusters size ~20, N=1000 => 200k*20 ~ 4M ops per seed; times 1000 seeds = 4B. GPU helps.\n\nAlternative parallelize member dimensions and candidates simultaneously.\n\nMain design options:\nA) One kernel per addition: grid over candidates, each thread computes max distance over members sequentially (or block reduce). That's launch overhead per addition per seed if seeds serialized - too slow. Better group seeds into blocks.\n\nB) Kernel where each CUDA block handles a distinct seed and generates its full candidate cluster sequentially in-block, parallelizing over candidates using block threads. Block count = #unclustered seeds. Each block has e.g. 256 threads, each thread handles a subset of candidate points; for each addition step, compute max distance across members (each thread loops over member list) and reduce to find best candidate. This yields parallelism within seed and across seeds, one kernel launch per outer clustering iteration. This is straightforward and correct.\n\nData:\n- points (double x,y arrays or double2)\n- clustered flags (uint8)\n- unclustered seed list maybe just scan all N with clustered flag, need preserve seed order? For best selection with ties, global best cardinality: we can use atomicMax on a 64-bit key encoding (cardinality << 32 | (N-1-seed)) to pick max cardinality then min seed. But output cluster membership depends on chosen best_seed and candidate members. Need store candidate members per seed? Could recompute for best seed or store all candidate clusters. Storing all N * cluster size ints potentially N^2 worst case. Better: kernel first finds best seed and cardinality (one pass computes full candidate clusters per block, reduces best key). Then a second call (or second phase) regenerates the best cluster with single block, writes members. This duplicates computation for the chosen seed only. Or store candidate clusters to a buffer of size N * N? Could be huge. Alternative store per-seed member lists in global memory allocated N*max members? Not safe.\n\nOption: In kernel, each block generates its candidate cluster and keeps members in shared memory (max N ints = 4N bytes; N=10000 -> 40KB, feasible but with 256 threads). Then after determining global best seed? The block that has best cluster can write its members to output if we know it's best, but best is only known after all blocks finish. Could use ability: each block compares atomicMax; need second kernel to copy members from shared impossible. Could have each block write its members to global buffer indexed by seed, requiring N*N ints, which for N=10k is 400MB, maybe okay but wasteful; for 100k impossible.\n\nBetter two-phase per outer iteration:\n- Phase 1 kernel: one block per candidate seed computes cardinality only (does not need store all members, but needs in_cluster flags). We need in_cluster per seed: N bytes per block. Could allocate global scratch [numSeeds][N] bytes: N^2 bytes. At N=10k 100MB, okay; at 100k 10GB not. Could instead use a per-block shared bitmap of N bits = N/8 bytes. For N=10000, 1250 bytes; for N=100k, 12.5KB okay. Since each block handles one seed, use shared bitset. Candidate members list in shared memory (N ints) also fine for 10k (40KB). Actually if we only compute cardinality, we don't need member list? We need distances to members; store members list in shared (N ints). Max shared 48KB default, can opt-in to 100KB+ on 3090 (Ampere 99KB). N can be maybe up to 100k → 400KB too big. But benchmark likely N around 1k-10k. We should design scalable: store members list in global scratch per block? Could use shared for modest N and fallback. Hmm.\n\nWe can optimize distance computation with precomputed distance matrix? That changes complexity and memory. But could avoid sqrt and repeated distance. For correctness exact: distance uses sqrt(dx^2+dy^2) in double. GPU double sqrt is correctly rounded? CUDA sqrt(double) should match CPU sqrt correctly rounded per IEEE (likely). To be safe, precompute pairwise distances on GPU? Same issue. We can compute on the fly identical formula. CUDA compilation with -use_fast_math would break; don't use.\n\nTie semantics: findClosestPoint selects candidate with max_dist < threshold and max_dist < min_diameter, scanning ascending. So among candidates with equal max_dist, first (smallest index) wins. GPU reduction must replicate smallest index tie. Use pair (min_diameter, candidate index) with comparison dist < min_diameter OR (dist == min_diameter && candidate < best). Wait scanning with strict update: if equal, does not update, so earlier candidate remains. So choose smaller index on tie. For max cardinality across seeds: strict >, seeds ascending by unclustered order, choose first seed in unclustered_indices order. unclustered_indices always ascending after removals (initialized ascending, removed preserving order). So min seed index on tie. Good.\n\nImportant: `clustered` snapshot at start of outer iteration; candidate generation does not update clustered. Fine.\n\nWe can compute cardinality per seed in parallel. Then choose best seed. Then need regenerate its members. Could regenerate in a second kernel with one block, using same semantics. Regenerating on GPU single block kernel is fine; then copy.\n\nAlternatively, during phase 1, if we store each block's members in shared we cannot retrieve after. But we could use \"last block done\" pattern: after atomicMax determines best key, each block checks whether its key equals global best at end; if so writes its shared members to global output. Problem: a block may check before later block finds higher key. Use a counter of finished blocks / threadfence. Standard last-block reduction: each block writes its candidate info to global, then increments counter; last block determines winner. But other blocks' member lists are gone. Unless each block writes its members to per-seed global scratch only if potential? Can't know. But could write members to global scratch indexed by seed — N^2 ints memory. Could write only the member list of each block to a compaction? Hmm.\n\nTwo-phase approach is simpler and regeneration cost is just one extra seed cluster (small). Do that.\n\nActually we can combine: phase1 blocks each compute cardinality and keep members in shared. Use global buffer of size (max blocks?) Hmm no.\n\nAlternative: one block per seed can atomically decide best as it finishes, and if it's currently best, write its members to global \"best members\" buffer along with key. Race: a later better block overwrites; final best is correct if ordering via atomicMax on key and separate write race. Use atomicMax returns old; if new key > old, block knows it improved best (but another concurrent better block could interleave). Suppose A sets key K1 returns old, starts writing members; B sets K2>K1 returns K1, writes members; B writes after A? Not guaranteed. Could use a mutex / atomic CAS to gate writes, and clear buffer carefully. Use key encoding includes seed index and cardinality. Standard approach: after atomicMax on 64-bit key returning old value, only the block that set the final maximum needs to write. But if another block later sets a higher value, its write supersedes. Ordering issue: block B (higher) might write members first, then block A (lower) writes after, corrupting. Let's think if there is algorithmic optimization preserving exact semantics.\n\nFor a given seed, cluster growth adds the point minimizing max distance to current cluster, subject to max < threshold. This is equivalent to finding a point whose distance to all current members < threshold and minimal max-distance. In other words, among points in the intersection of balls of radius threshold around all members, pick the one minimizing max distance. This is a \"minimax\" selection. Hard to accelerate beyond distance checks.\n\nBut we can note that the max distance to cluster equals max over members; if we maintain for each candidate its current max distance to the growing cluster, when a new member is added we only need to update each candidate's max with distance to the new member. Then each addition requires N distance computations (one per candidate) instead of N*m. This is a huge algorithmic improvement! And exact same result because max over members = running max updated incrementally. But careful: candidate set excludes clustered/in_cluster. We can maintain an array `maxDist[candidate]` for all candidates. Initially for seed cluster, maxDist[c]=distance(c, seed) for all candidates. Then each addition: scan candidates, skip clustered/in_cluster; eligible if maxDist[c] < threshold; pick min maxDist with tie smallest index; add; then update maxDist[c] = max(maxDist[c], distance(c,newMember)) for all c (including? for all candidates except in-cluster/clustered, but can update all). This reduces per addition from O(N*m) to O(N), and total per seed from O(N*m^2-ish) to O(N*m). Since m additions ~ cluster size, work per seed O(N*m), same as before first addition but no m factor. Actually original total per seed = sum_{k=1..m} N*k = O(N m^2). Incremental is O(N m). Huge speedup.\n\nCan we parallelize this? Within a block per seed, maintain `maxDist` for its candidates. Is it semantically identical? Let's carefully verify against original.\n\nOriginal per addition recomputes `max_dist = max_{member in cluster} distance(candidate, member)`, starting from 0.0 (max_dist initialized 0.0; note if cluster nonempty, max over distances; distances >=0). In incremental, for seed cluster `maxDist(c)=dist(c,seed)`. After adding point p, new max = max(old max, dist(c,p)). Exactly max over all members. With equal no change; value same. Our update `if (d > maxDist) maxDist = d` also equal no change. Value exact same.\n\nEligibility: original `max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter`. Good.\n\nAnother subtlety: original sets `in_cluster[seed] = true` before finding; candidates skip in-cluster. {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1792437012058,"sessionID":"ses_eaa7a1671ffe8yveet5e9w9qDM","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call_00_binlhxyd8gqwftvsp3ygbwov","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/qtclustering_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_cuda_r1/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp","content":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - CUDA Parallel Version\n//\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy:\n//   * Every unclustered point is evaluated as a seed simultaneously.\n//   * One CUDA thread block owns one seed and grows that seed's candidate\n//     cluster cooperatively (all candidates in the block are scanned in\n//     parallel for every greedy addition).\n//   * Candidate selection across seeds uses an atomic max over a packed\n//     (cardinality, seed) key, which reproduces the original tie-breaking\n//     (largest cardinality, then smallest seed index).\n//   * The winning cluster is regenerated by a single block and copied back.\n//\n// The per-candidate maximum distance to the cluster is maintained\n// incrementally: after a point is added, every remaining candidate only\n// needs to be compared against that one new member.  This is exactly the\n// same value the sequential version recomputes from scratch, so the\n// resulting clusters are bit-for-bit identical.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdint>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n#define QT_CUDA_CHECK(expr)                                                        \\\n    do {                                                                           \\\n        cudaError_t qt_err = (expr);                                               \\\n        if (qt_err != cudaSuccess) {                                               \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error (%s) at %s:%d\\n\",                          \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(qt_err), __FILE__, __LINE__);               \\\n            std::exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                          \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points (device)\n__device__ __forceinline__ double distance(const double2& p1, const double2& p2) {\n    const double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    const double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Grow a candidate cluster starting at `seed`.\n// `maxDist[c]` holds the maximum distance from candidate c to the current\n// cluster members.  Returns the number of members (written to `members`).\n__device__ __forceinline__ int growCandidateCluster(\n        const int seed,\n        const double2* __restrict__ points,\n        const uint8_t* __restrict__ clustered,\n        const int point_count,\n        const double threshold,\n        double* __restrict__ maxDist,\n        int* __restrict__ members,\n        unsigned int* __restrict__ in_cluster_bits,\n        double* __restrict__ warp_dist,\n        int* __restrict__ warp_idx,\n        int* __restrict__ best_shared) {\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int lane = tid & 31;\n    const int warp = tid >> 5;\n\n    // Clear the membership bitset.\n    const int bit_words = (point_count + 31) >> 5;\n    for (int i = tid; i < bit_words; i += blockDim.x) {\n        in_cluster_bits[i] = 0u;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    if (tid == 0) {\n        members[0] = seed;\n        in_cluster_bits[seed >> 5] |= (1u << (seed & 31));\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    // Initialise every candidate's maximum distance to the seed.\n    const double2 seed_point = points[seed];\n    for (int c = tid; c < point_count; c += blockDim.x) {\n        if (clustered[c] || (in_cluster_bits[c >> 5] & (1u << (c & 31)))) {\n            // Already clustered or the seed itself: permanently ineligible.\n            maxDist[c] = threshold;\n        } else {\n            maxDist[c] = distance(points[c], seed_point);\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    int member_count = 1;\n\n    while (member_count < point_count) {\n        // Pick the eligible candidate with the smallest maximum distance.\n        // Ties are resolved in favour of the smaller point index, which\n        // matches the sequential scan order.\n        double local_dist = 1.0e300;\n        int local_idx = -1;\n\n        for (int c = tid; c < point_count; c += blockDim.x) {\n            const double d = maxDist[c];\n            if (d >= threshold) continue;                 // permanently ineligible\n            if (clustered[c]) continue;\n            if (in_cluster_bits[c >> 5] & (1u << (c & 31))) continue;\n            if (d < local_dist || (d == local_dist && c < local_idx)) {\n                local_dist = d;\n                local_idx = c;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Warp-level reduction.\n        #pragma unroll\n        for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {\n            const double other_dist = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, local_dist, offset);\n            const int other_idx = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, local_idx, offset);\n            if (other_idx >= 0 &&\n                (other_dist < local_dist ||\n                 (other_dist == local_dist && other_idx < local_idx))) {\n                local_dist = other_dist;\n                local_idx = other_idx;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (lane == 0) {\n            warp_dist[warp] = local_dist;\n            warp_idx[warp] = local_idx;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (warp == 0) {\n            const int num_warps = (blockDim.x + 31) >> 5;\n            double block_dist = (lane < num_warps) ? warp_dist[lane] : 1.0e300;\n            int block_idx = (lane < num_warps) ? warp_idx[lane] : -1;\n\n            #pragma unroll\n            for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {\n                const double other_dist = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, block_dist, offset);\n                const int other_idx = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, block_idx, offset);\n                if (other_idx >= 0 &&\n                    (other_dist < block_dist ||\n                     (other_dist == block_dist && other_idx < block_idx))) {\n                    block_dist = other_dist;\n                    block_idx = other_idx;\n                }\n            }\n            if (lane == 0) {\n                *best_shared = block_idx;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int best = *best_shared;\n        if (best < 0) break;\n\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            members[member_count] = best;\n            in_cluster_bits[best >> 5] |= (1u << (best & 31));\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        // Fold the newly added member into every remaining candidate's\n        // running maximum distance.\n        const double2 new_member = points[best];\n        for (int c = tid; c < point_count; c += blockDim.x) {\n            const double old_dist = maxDist[c];\n            if (old_dist >= threshold) continue;\n            if (clustered[c]) continue;\n            if (in_cluster_bits[c >> 5] & (1u << (c & 31))) continue;\n            const double new_dist = distance(points[c], new_member);\n            if (new_dist > old_dist) {\n                maxDist[c] = new_dist;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        ++member_count;\n    }\n\n    return member_count;\n}\n\n// One block per seed; scratch storage lives in dynamic shared memory.\ntemplate <bool WRITE_MEMBERS>\n__global__ void candidateKernelShared(\n        const double2* __restrict__ points,\n        const uint8_t* __restrict__ clustered,\n        const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n        int num_seeds,\n        int point_count,\n        double threshold,\n        unsigned long long* __restrict__ best_key,\n        int* __restrict__ out_members,\n        int* __restrict__ out_count,\n        uint8_t* __restrict__ d_clustered) {\n    (void)num_seeds;\n\n    extern __shared__ unsigned char shared_raw[];\n    double* maxDist = reinterpret_cast<double*>(shared_raw);\n    int* members = reinterpret_cast<int*>(maxDist + point_count);\n    unsigned int* in_cluster_bits =\n        reinterpret_cast<unsigned int*>(members + point_count);\n\n    __shared__ double warp_dist[32];\n    __shared__ int warp_idx[32];\n    __shared__ int best_shared;\n\n    const int seed = seeds[blockIdx.x];\n    const int count = growCandidateCluster(\n        seed, points, clustered, point_count, threshold,\n        maxDist, members, in_cluster_bits,\n        warp_dist, warp_idx, &best_shared);\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    if (WRITE_MEMBERS) {\n        for (int i = tid; i < count; i += blockDim.x) {\n            const int member = members[i];\n            out_members[i] = member;\n            d_clustered[member] = 1;\n        }\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            *out_count = count;\n        }\n    } else {\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            const unsigned long long key =\n                (static_cast<unsigned long long>(static_cast<unsigned>(count)) << 32) |\n                static_cast<unsigned>(0xFFFFFFFFu - static_cast<unsigned>(seed));\n            atomicMax(best_key, key);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Persistent-grid variant used when the per-seed working set does not fit\n// in shared memory.  Each resident block reuses its own global scratch area.\ntemplate <bool WRITE_MEMBERS>\n__global__ void candidateKernelGlobal(\n        const double2* __restrict__ points,\n        const uint8_t* double* maxDist = scratch_dist + block_offset * point_count;\n    int* members = scratch_members + block_offset * point_count;\n    unsigned int* in_cluster_bits = scratch_bits + block_offset * bit_words;\n\n    __shared__ double warp_dist[32];\n    __shared__ int warp_idx[32];\n    __shared__ int best_shared;\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n\n    for (int seed_index = blockIdx.x; seed_index < num_seeds; seed_index += gridDim.x) {\n        const int seed = seeds[seed_index];\n        const int count = growCandidateCluster(\n            seed, points, clustered, point_count, threshold,\n            maxDist, members, in_cluster_bits,\n            warp_dist, warp_idx, &best_shared);\n\n        if (WRITE_MEMBERS) {\n            for (int i = tid; i < count; i += blockDim.x) {\n                const int member = members[i];\n                out_members[i] = member;\n                d_clustered[member] = 1;\n            }\n            if (tid == 0) {\n                *out_count = count;\n            }\n        } else {\n            if (tid == 0) {\n                const unsigned long long key =\n                    (static_cast<unsigned long long>(static_cast<unsigned>(count)) << 32) |\n                    static_cast<unsigned>(0xFFFFFFFFu - static_cast<unsigned>(seed));\n                atomicMax(best_key, key);\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (CUDA)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    if (N == 0) {\n        return clusters;\n    }\n\n    // Upload points as                    sizeof(int) * N));\n\n    const int block_size = 256;\n    const int bit_words = (N + 31) >> 5;\n    const size_t shared_bytes =\n        static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(double) +\n QT_CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncSetAttribute(\n            candidateKernelShared<false>,\n            cudaFuncAttributeMaxDynamicSharedMemorySize,\n            static_cast<int>(shared_bytes)));\n        QT_CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncSetAttribute(\n            candidateKernelShared<true>,\n            cudaFuncAttributeMaxDynamicSharedMemorySize,\n            static_cast<int>(shared_bytes)));\n    }\n\n    // Global-scratch fallback allocations (only needed for very large N).\n    double* scratch_dist = nullptr;\n    int* scratch_members = nullptr;\n    unsigned int* scratch_bits = nullptr;\n    int persistent_blocks = 0;\n    if (!use_shared) {\n        const size_t per_block =\n            static_cast<size_t>(N) * (sizeof(double) + sizeof(int)) +\n            static_cast<size_t>(bit_words) *    QT_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch_members,\n                                 sizeof(int) * static_cast<size_t>(persistent_blocks) * N));\n        QT_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch_bits,\n                                 sizeof(unsigned int) * static_cast<size_t>(persistent_blocks) * bit_words));\n    }\n\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const int num_seeds = best_seed =\n            static_cast<int>(0xFFFFFFFFu - static_cast<unsigned>(key & 0xFFFFFFFFu));\n\n        if (best_seed < 0 || best_seed >= N) {\n            break;\n        }\n\n        //
